# Misuzu — current still-image observation index

All 57 listed image presentations and all 565 panels were inspected as stills. This distinguishes actual structured tool returns from assistant-authored notes. It does not certify audio, continuous motion or truth of perception. Raw response files remain in the full rebuild work root under `observations/misuzu/raw-receipts/`; their hashes and exact presentation events are embedded below. The raw images themselves are not embedded in this Markdown-only packet.

Observation inputs were frozen before claim finalization; no note is superseded. Hash references identify files and do not make the assistant's observation independently verified. See [claim review](FULL_REBUILD_CLAIM_REVIEW.md) and [formal accounting](FULL_REBUILD_REVIEW_ACCOUNTING.md).

## Authored notes and portable index

The fenced data below preserves all 22 authored note files, their hashes, exact event metadata and the frozen-file inventory. Early notes use one presentation per observation object; later notes list presentations at the top and group observations by source. These schemas cover every event exactly once. The 18 CIDOL files and acquisition/hash/git-blob records remain at `observations/misuzu/locked-scripts/`; Dear files and their earlier acquisition record remain in the prior execution's `work/misuzu/a1/`. Selected text locators and hashes are in [FULL_REBUILD_TEXT_LOCATORS.md](FULL_REBUILD_TEXT_LOCATORS.md).

            "observation": "The VEIL commu survey moves between clubroom and practice room.102.1333 joined-hands close-up calls the project important.183.8333 Producer says present Misuzu cannot yet master the new song;265.55 has Misuzu's confidence beside a chin-hand close smile;306.4 explicitly describes an unfinished difficult piece incorporating huge growth.347.25/388.1 retain a modest held-forearm or hands-lowered body presentation while the text places present song/performer near top-idol competition.428.95 is eyes-closed near portrait directing attention to the rival beside her.",
            "claims": [
              "019",
              "020",

              "044",
              "045"

            "limit": "Difficulty and incompletion are freshly visible textual controls, not measured vocal/choreographic difficulty or proof of actual culmination. Recurring portraits cannot certify achieved benevolent sovereignty."
          }
        ],

      }

    {
      "path": "observations/misuzu/notes-survey-M01-M16.json",
      "sha256": "d41de0923ace56ad078b3159909ef7513d513aefcac939cef5add571cf9a1536",
      "record": {
        "schema": "misuzu.full_rebuild.observed_still_notes.v1",
        "modality": "visual_stills",
        "observations": [
          {
            "presentation_id": "MZ-FULL-SURVEY-M01-01",
            "source_alias": "M01",
            "actually_viewed": "All12 panels in the original-detail contact sheet.",
            "findings": [
              {
                "frame_ids": [
                  "F000001",
                  "F000002",
                  "F000003",
                  "F000004",
                  "F000005",
                  "F000006"
                ],
                "observation": "A vertical story viewport is surrounded by wide, repeated light-colored uploader artwork. Misuzu is shown in tea-room, bench, gym and classroom settings, with hand-to-face or hands-near-chest poses and both closed/open eyes. F000004 at851.1s visibly states dissatisfaction that her friends are self-sufficient; the face is a slight smile, not an overt grimace.",
                "limit": "The sidebars occupy much of each full frame and therefore contaminate any whole-frame brightness comparison with later differently formatted compilations. The care statement requires targeted context; a smiling face alone does not prove benevolence."
              },
              {
                "frame_ids": [
                  "F000007",
                  "F000008",
                  "F000009",
                  "F000010",
                  "F000011",
                  "F000012"
                ],
                "observation": "Temari and a visual trainer receive separate portrait frames. Misuzu later appears with folded arms and in a sunset exterior. F000012 at2796.4s is an illustration/credits end section, outside the retained Dear010 operational endpoint2753.2s.",
                "limit": "All12 are survey points of the complete source, but the endcard is not new Dear dialogue evidence. Other characters' portraits and utterances reinforce that source metrics are not Misuzu-only."
              }
            ],
            "claim_ids": [
              "MZ-AV-001",
              "MZ-AV-006",
              "MZ-AV-007",
              "MZ-AV-041"
            ],
            "auditory_observations": [],
            "interpretation": "The wide uploader border makes a simple early-versus-late full-frame luma narrative especially unsafe. F000004 renews a precise care/self-reward conjunction pending the targeted comparison."
          },
          {
            "presentation_id": "MZ-FULL-SURVEY-M16-01",
            "source_alias": "M16",
            "actually_viewed": "All12 panels in the original-detail contact sheet.",
            "findings": [
              {
                "frame_ids": [
                  "F000001",
                  "F000003",
                  "F000004",
                  "F000005",
                  "F000006"
                ],
                "observation": "A disc-like mechanical structure appears against a blue nebula; a tiny central figure is placed within a tall mechanical doorway; another sample shows a long symmetric dark corridor; a bright green/purple wireframe-like architectural image follows; orbital rings surround the central figure between large faceted structures.",
                "limit": "These are sampled authored constructions. The images do not prove camera travel or any mechanical object's motion."
              },
              {
                "frame_ids": [
                  "F000002",
                  "F000007",
                  "F000008",
                  "F000009",
                  "F000010",
                  "F000011",
                  "F000012"
                ],
                "observation": "Close illustrated mouth/profile/front-face crops contrast with pointed mechanical forms, bright beam-like lines and a pink luminous broken/faceted central structure. F000008 shows a smiling figure with a dark-gloved hand beside the face.",
                "limit": "Beam/fragment imagery visually complicates an exclusively restful or shelter-like reading. It does not establish musical aggression, performed danger, violence in narrative continuity or animation speed."
              }
            ],
            "claim_ids": [
              "MZ-AV-019",
              "MZ-AV-025",
              "MZ-AV-029",
              "MZ-AV-040",
              "MZ-AV-044",
              "MZ-AV-045"
            ],
            "auditory_observations": [],
            "interpretation": "The authored VEIL field includes technological enclosure, extreme scale and beam/fragment motifs as well as celestial imagery and intimate portraiture. It warrants a more specific visual account than generic soft night-sky vastness. None of those images resolves the benevolent-plurality question."
          }
        ],
        "trust_boundary": "Assistant-authored observations of actual presented stills. Tool returns establish only what image bytes were returned; they do not prove perception or certify listening/continuous motion."
      }
